y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions\ndouble computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n    \n    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);\n    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);\n    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);\n    \n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const double cv = c[idx];\n                \n                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) \n                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                         - gamma * const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * \n                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int         printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: dy, dz, \n                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n        \n        // Update concentration\n        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(cold, cnew);\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions\ndouble computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t zp = // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cej5K1otdW1CWgF7iZFMX","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01MLuxW5q4yZ6NgMV2xnzYSm","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/cahn-hilliard_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_hybrid_r2/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Query for CUDA-aware MPI support (Open MPI extension)\n#if defined(OPEN_MPI) && OPEN_MPI\n#include <mpi-ext.h>\n#endif\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA implementation.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * MPI  : 1D domain decomposition along Z. Each rank owns a contiguous slab\n//            of Z planes and drives one GPU. A halo of 2 planes on each side is\n//            exchanged once per time step, which is enough to compute the\n//            chemical potential in the first ghost plane locally and therefore\n//            to evaluate the second (update) stencil on all owned cells.\n//   * CUDA : both stencil sweeps run as shared-memory tiled kernels that march\n//            along Z keeping the Z-neighbours in registers.\n//   * OpenMP: host side work (field initialization, validation reductions,\n//            halo staging) is threaded.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                               \\\n    do {                                                                                                               \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                               \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                                     \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);                  \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                              \\\n        }                                                                                                              \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Tile geometry for the stencil kernels\n#define TX 32\n#define TY 8\n\nenum StencilMode { MODE_MU = 0, MODE_UPDATE = 1 };\n\n// Fused tiled stencil kernel.\n//   MODE_MU     : out = chemical potential of \"in\" (== c)\n//   MODE_UPDATE : out = aux + dtD * laplacian(in) (in == mu, aux == c)\n//\n// Local Z indices run over [0, nzloc+4); owned planes are [2, nzloc+2).\n// The global Z coordinate of local plane k is gzoff + k, which drives the\n// clamped boundary condition at the physical domain boundaries.\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(TX* TY) void stencilKernel(const double* __restrict__ in, const double* __restrict__ aux,\n                                                        double* __restrict__ out, const int nx, const int ny,\n                                                        const int kbeg, const int kend, const int kchunk,\n                                                        const long gzoff, const long nzg, const double idx2,\n                                                        const double idy2, const double idz2, const double gamma,\n                                                        const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n                                                        const double dtD) {\n    __shared__ double s[TY + 2][TX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * TX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * TY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still load (clamped) values so that they feed\n    // the correct halo entries to their in-range neighbours.\n    const int xc = min(x, nx - 1);\n    const int yc = min(y, ny - 1);\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(yc) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n\n    const int k0 = kbeg + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * kchunk;\n    const int k1 = min(k0 + kchunk, kend);\n    if (k0 >= k1) return;\n\n    const int xm = max(xc - 1, 0);\n    const int xp = min(xc + 1, nx - 1);\n    const size_t rowm = static_cast<size_t>(max(yc - 1, 0)) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n    const size_t rowp = static_cast<size_t>(min(yc + 1, ny - 1)) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n\n    double cm = in[static_cast<size_t>(k0 - 1) * plane + row + xc];\n    double c0 = in[static_cast<size_t>(k0) * plane + row + xc];\n\n    for (int k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n        const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(k) * plane;\n        const double cp = in[base + plane + row + xc];\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = c0;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[base + row + xm];\n        if (tx == TX - 1) s[ty + 1][TX + 1] = in[base + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[base + rowm + xc];\n        if (ty == TY - 1) s[TY + 1][tx + 1] = in[base + rowp + xc];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const long gz = gzoff + k;\n        const double up = (gz < nzg - 1) ? cp : c0;\n        const double dn = (gz > 0) ? cm : c0;\n\n        const double cxx = (s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * c0) * idx2;\n        const double cyy = (s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * c0) * idy2;\n        const double czz = (up + dn - 2.0 * c0) * idz2;\n        const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n        if (active) {\n            const size_t o = base + static_cast<size_t>(y) * static_cast<size_t>(nx) + static_cast<size_t>(x);\n            if (MODE == MODE_MU) {\n                out[o] = 4.5 * ((c0 + 1.0) * e_AA + (c0 - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * c0 * e_AB) + 3.0 * c0 + c0 * c0 * c0 -\n                         gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[o] = aux[o] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n\n        cm = c0;\n        c0 = cp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize the local slab of the concentration field (host, OpenMP threaded)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t z0, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nzloc; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = (z + z0) * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = 1;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable           const double idy2, const double idz2, const double gamma,\n                                                        const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n                                                        const double dtD) {\n    __shared__ double s[TY + 2][TX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * return;\n\n    const int xm = max(xc - 1, 0);\n    const int xp = min(xc + 1, nx - 1);\n    const size_t rowm = static_cast<size_t>(max(yc - 1, 0)) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n    const size_t rowp = static_cast<size_t>(min(yc + 1, ny - 1)) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n\n    double cm = in[static_cast<size_t>(k0 - 1) * plane + row + xc];\n    double c0 = in[static_cast<size_t>(k0) * plane + row + xc];\n\n    for (int k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n        const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(k) * plane;\n        const double cp = in[base + plane + row + xc];\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = c0;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[base + row + xm];\n        if (tx == TX - 1) s[ty + 1][TX + 1] = in[base + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[base + rowm + xc];\n        if (ty == TY - 1) s[TY + 1][tx + 1] = in[base + rowp + xc];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const long gz = gzoff + k;\n        const double up = (gz < nzg - 1) ? cp : c0;\n        const double dn = (gz > 0) ? cm : c0;\n\n        const double cxx = (s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * c0) * idx2;\n        const double cyy = (s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * c0) * idy2;\n        const double czz = (up + dn - 2.0 * c0) * idz2;\n        const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n        if (active) {\n            const size_t o = base + static_cast<size_t>(y) * static_cast<size_t>(nx) + static_cast<size_t>(x);\n            if (MODE == MODE_MU) {\n                out[o] = 4.5 * ((c0 + 1.0) * e_AA + (c0 - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * c0 * e_AB) + 3.0 * c0 + c0 * c0 * c0 -\n                         gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[o] = aux[o] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n\n        cm = c0;\n        c0 = cp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize the local slab of the concentration field (host, OpenMP threaded)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t z0, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nzloc; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = (z + z0) * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = 1;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int worldRank = 0;\n    int worldSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);\n\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (worldRank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (worldRank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (worldRank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ?                  \\\n        }                                                                                                              \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Tile geometry for the stencil kernels\n#define TX 32\n#define TY 8\n\nenum StencilMode { MODE_MU = 0, MODE_UPDATE = 1 };\n\n// Fused tiled stencil kernel.\n//   MODE_MU     : out = chemical potential of \"in\" (== c)\n//   MODE_UPDATE : out = aux + dtD * laplacian(in) (in == mu, aux == c)\n//\n// Local Z indices run over [0, nzloc+4); owned planes are [2, nzloc+2).\n// The global Z coordinate of local plane k is gzoff + k, which drives the\n// clamped boundary condition at the physical domain boundaries.\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(TX* TY) void stencilKernel(const double* __restrict__ in, const double* __restrict__ aux,\n                                                        double* __restrict__ out, const int nx, const int ny,\n                                                        const int kbeg, const int kend, const int kchunk,\n                                                        const long gzoff, const long nzg, const double idx2,\n                                                        const double idy2, const double idz2, const double gamma,\n                                                        const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n                                                        const double dtD) {\n    __shared__ double s[TY + 2][TX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * TX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * TY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still load (clamped) values so that they feed\n    // the correct halo entries to their in-range neighbours.\n    const int xc = min(x, nx - 1);\n    const int yc = min(y, ny - 1);\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(yc) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n\n    const int k0 = kbeg + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * kchunk;\n    const int k1 = min(k0 + kchunk, kend);\n    if (k0 >= k1) return;\n\n    const int xm = max(xc - 1, 0);\n    const int xp = min(xc + 1, nx - 1);\n    const size_t rowm = static_cast<size_t>(max(yc - 1, 0)) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n    const size_t rowp = static_cast<size_t>(min(yc + 1, ny - 1)) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n\n    double cm = in[static_cast<size_t>(k0 - 1) * plane + row + xc];\n    double c0 = in[static_cast<size_t>(k0) * plane + row + xc];\n\n    for (int k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n        const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(k) * plane;\n        const double cp = in[base + plane + row + xc];\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = c0;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[base + row + xm];\n        if (tx == TX - 1) s[ty + 1][TX + 1] = in[base + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[base + rowm + xc];\n        if (ty == TY - 1) s[TY + 1][tx + 1] = in[base + rowp + xc];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const long gz = gzoff + k;\n        const double up = (gz < nzg - 1) ? cp : c0;\n        const double dn = (gz > 0) ? cm : c0;\n\n        const double cxx = (s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * c0) * idx2;\n        const double cyy = (s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * c0) * idy2;\n        const double czz = (up + dn - 2.0 * c0) * idz2;\n        const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n        if (active) {\n            const size_t o = base + static_cast<size_t>(y) * static_cast<size_t>(nx) + static_cast<size_t>(x);\n            if (MODE == MODE_MU) {\n                out[o] = 4.5 * ((c0 + 1.0) * e_AA + (c0 - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * c0 * e_AB) + 3.0 * c0 + c0 * c0 * c0 -\n                         gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[o] = aux[o] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n\n        cm = c0;\n        c0 = cp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize the local slab of the concentration field (host, OpenMP threaded)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t z0, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nzloc; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = (z + z0) * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = 1;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int worldRank = 0;\n    int worldSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);\n\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (worldRank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (worldRank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (worldRank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // ---- MPI decomposition along Z ---------------------------------------\n    // Every active rank needs at least 2 owned planes so that a 2-plane halo\n    // can be served by its immediate neighbour.\n    int nActive = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), std::max<size_t>(nz / 2, 1)));\n    const bool activeRank = (worldRank < nActive);\n\n    MPI_Comm simComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, activeRank ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED, worldRank, &simComm);\n\n    size_t z0 = 0;\n    size_t nzloc = 0;\n    std::vector<int> allCounts(worldSize, 0);\n    std::vector<int> allDispls(worldSize, 0);\n    {\n        // Block distribution of nz planes over nActive ranks\n        size_t off = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < nActive; ++r) {\n            const size_t cnt = nz / nActive + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < nz % nActive ? 1 : 0);\n            if (r == worldRank) {\n                z0 = off;\n                nzloc = cnt;\n            }\n            allCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(cnt * nx * ny);\n            allDispls[r] = static_cast<int>(off * nx * ny);\n            off += cnt;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---- GPU selection: one GPU per rank, round-robin within the node -----\n    if (activeRank) {\n        MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n        MPI_Comm_split_type(simComm, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, 0, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n        int nodeRank = 0;\n        MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n        MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n        int devCount = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devCount));\n        if (devCount == 0) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available\\n\");\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(nodeRank % devCount));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr)); // establish context before timing\n    }\n\n    if (worldRank == 0) {\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d (active: %d), OpenMP threads/rank: %d\\n\", worldSize, nActive, omp_get_max_threads());\n        printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // ---- Allocation -------------------------------------------------------\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t nzalloc = nzloc + 4;           // 2 ghost planes on each side\n    const size_t localBytes = plane * nzalloc * sizeof(double);\n    const size_t ownedElems = plane * nzloc;\n\n    double* d_c = nullptr;\n    double* d_cnew = nullptr;\n    double* d_mu = nullptr;\n    double *h_sendLo = nullptr, *h_sendHi = nullptr, *h_recvLo = nullptr, *h_recvHi = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t s0 = nullptr, s1 = nullptr;\n    cudaEvent_t evMu = nullptr;\n\n    std::vector<double> hostSlab;\n\n    bool cudaAwareMPI = false;\n#if defined(MPIX_CUDA_AWARE_SUPPORT) && MPIX_CUDA_AWARE_SUPPORT\n    cudaAwareMPI = (MPIX_Query_cuda_support() == 1);\n#endif\n\n    int left = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    int right = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n\n    if (activeRank) {\n        int simRank = 0, simSize = 1;\n        MPI_Comm_rank(simComm, &simRank);\n        MPI_Comm_size(simComm, &simSize);\n        left = (simRank > 0) ? simRank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n        right = (simRank + 1 < simSize) ? simRank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_c, localBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cnew, localBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mu, localBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_c, 0, localBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_cnew, 0, localBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_mu, 0, localBytes));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&s0));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&s1));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&evMu, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n\n        if (!cudaAwareMPI && simSize > 1) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_sendLo, 2 * plane * sizeof(double)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_sendHi, 2 * plane * sizeof(double)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recvLo, 2 * plane * sizeof(double)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recvHi, 2 * plane * sizeof(double)));\n        }\n\n        // Initialize on the host (OpenMP) and upload the owned slab\n        hostSlab.resize(ownedElems);\n        initializeConcentration(hostSlab, nx, ny, nz, z0, nzloc);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_c + 2 * plane, hostSlab.data(), ownedElems * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n\n    if (worldRank == 0) printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n\n    // ---- Kernel launch configuration -------------------------------------\n    const double idx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);\n    const double idy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);\n    const double idz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);\n    const double dtD = dt * D;\n    const long gzoff = static_cast<long>(z0) - 2;\n    const long nzg = static_cast<long>(nz);\n\n    const int nxi = static_cast<int>(nx);\n    const int nyi = static_cast<int>(ny);\n    const int nzl = static_cast<int>(nzloc);\n\n    const dim3 block(TX, TY, 1);\n    const int gx = (nxi + TX - 1) / TX;\n    const int gy = (nyi + TY - 1) / TY;\n\n    // Choose the Z chunk per block so that enough blocks are in flight\n    int kchunkMu = 8;\n    if (activeRank) {\n        int smCount = 0;\n        int dev = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&dev));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&smCount, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, dev));\n        const int xyBlocks = gx * gy;\n        const int wanted = std::max(1, 8 * smCount / std::max(1, xyBlocks));\n        kchunkMu = std::max(1, std::min(32, (nzl + 3 + wanted - 1) / wanted));\n    }\n\n    // mu is additionally evaluated in the first ghost plane on each side that\n    // has a neighbour, so the update stencil can be applied to all owned cells.\n    const int muBeg = (left != MPI_PROC_NULL) ? 1 : 2;\n    const int muEnd = (right != MPI_PROC_NULL) ? nzl + 3 : nzl + 2;\n    // Overlap boundary/interior only when there is enough interior work\n    const bool splitUpdate = (left != MPI_PROC_NULL || right != MPI_PROC_NULL) && nzl >= 6;\n\n    const size_t haloElems = 2 * plane;\n    const size_t haloBytes = haloElems * sizeof(double);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    if (activeRank) {\n        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n            // --- chemical potential over owned + one ghost plane per side ---\n            {\n                const int span = muEnd - muBeg;\n                const dim3 grid(gx, gy, (span + kchunkMu - 1) / kchunkMu);\n                stencilKernel<MODE_MU><<<grid, block, 0, s0>>>(d_c, nullptr, d_mu, nxi, nyi, muBeg, muEnd, kchunkMu,\n                                                               gzoff, nzg, idx2, idy2, idz2, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB,\n                                                               dtD);\n            }\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(evMu, s0));\n\n            if (splitUpdate) {\n                // Boundary slabs first: they feed the halo exchange\n                const dim3 gb(gx, gy, 1);\n                stencilKernel<MODE_UPDATE><<<gb, block, 0, s0>>>(d_mu, d_c, d_cnew, nxi, nyi, 2, 4, 2, gzoff, nzg, idx2,\n                                                                 idy2, idz2, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, dtD);\n                stencilKernel<MODE_UPDATE><<<gb, block, 0, s0>>>(d_mu, d_c, d_cnew, nxi, nyi, nzl, nzl + 2, 2, gzoff,\n                                                                 nzg, idx2, idy2, idz2, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, dtD);\n                if (!cudaAwareMPI) {\n                    if (left != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_sendLo, d_cnew + 2 * plane, haloBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s0));\n                    if (right != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_sendHi, d_cnew + static_cast<size_t>(nzl) * plane, haloBytes,\n                                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s0));\n                }\n\n                // Interior update runs concurrently with the halo exchange\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(s1, evMu, 0));\n                const int span = nzl - 4;\n                const dim3 gi(gx, gy, (span + kchunkMu - 1) / kchunkMu);\n                stencilKernel<MODE_UPDATE><<<gi, block, 0, s1>>>(d_mu, d_c, d_cnew, nxi, nyi, 4, nzl, kchunkMu, gzoff,\n                                                                 nzg, idx2, idy2, idz2, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, dtD);\n\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s0));\n\n                if (cudaAwareMPI) {\n                    MPI_Sendrecv(d_cnew + 2 * plane, static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, left, 0,\n                                 d_cnew + static_cast<size_t>(nzl + 2) * plane, static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                                 right, 0, simComm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n                    MPI_Sendrecv(d_cnew + static_cast<size_t>(nzl) * plane, static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                                 right, 1, d_cnew, static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, left, 1, simComm,\n                                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n                } else {\n                    MPI_Sendrecv(h_sendLo, static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, left, 0, h_recvHi,\n                                 static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, right, 0, simComm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n                    MPI_Sendrecv(h_sendHi, static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, right, 1, h_recvLo,\n                                 static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, left, 1, simComm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n                    if (right != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_cnew + static_cast<size_t>(nzl + 2) * plane, h_recvHi, haloBytes,\n                                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s0));\n                    if (left != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_cnew, h_recvLo, haloBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s0));\n                }\n\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s1));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s0));\n            } else {\n                const int span = nzl;\n                const dim3 grid(gx, gy, (span + kchunkMu - 1) / kchunkMu);\n                stencilKernel<MODE_UPDATE><<<grid, block, 0, s0>>>(d_mu, d_c, d_cnew, nxi, nyi, 2, nzl + 2, kchunkMu,\n                                                                   gzoff, nzg, idx2, idy2, idz2, gamma, e_AA, e_BB,\n                                                                   e_AB, dtD);\n                if (left != MPI_PROC_NULL || right != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n                    if (cudaAwareMPI) {\n                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s0));\n                        MPI_Sendrecv(d_cnew + 2 * plane, static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, left, 0,\n                                     d_cnew + static_cast<size_t>(nzl + 2) * plane, static_cast<int>(haloElems),\n                                     MPI_DOUBLE, right, 0, simComm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n                        MPI_Sendrecv(d_cnew + static_cast<size_t>(nzl) * plane, static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                                     right, 1, d_cnew, static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, left, 1, simComm,\n                                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n                    } else {\n                        if (left != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                            CUDA_CHECK(\n                                cudaMemcpyAsync(h_sendLo, d_cnew + 2 * plane, haloBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s0));\n                        if (right != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_sendHi, d_cnew + static_cast<size_t>(nzl) * plane, haloBytes,\n                                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s0));\n                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s0));\n                        MPI_Sendrecv(h_sendLo, static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, left, 0, h_recvHi,\n                                     static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, right, 0, simComm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n                        MPI_Sendrecv(h_sendHi, static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, right, 1, h_recvLo,\n                                     static_cast<int>(haloElems), MPI_DOUBLE, left, 1, simComm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n                        if (right != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_cnew + static_cast<size_t>(nzl + 2) * plane, h_recvHi,\n                                                       haloBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s0));\n                        if (left != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_cnew, h_recvLo, haloBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s0));\n                    }\n                }\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s0));\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers\n            std::swap(d_c, d_cnew);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (worldRank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate performance\n        double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n        double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    }\n\n    // ---- Gather the full field on rank 0 when it is needed ----------------\n    std::vector<double> c;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        if (activeRank) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostSlab.data(), d_c + 2 * plane, ownedElems * sizeof(double),\n                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        }\n        if (worldRank == 0) c.resize(gridSize);\n        MPI_Gatherv(hostSlab.data(), static_cast<int>(ownedElems), MPI_DOUBLE, worldRank == 0 ? c.data() : nullptr,\n                    allCounts.data(), allDispls.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n\n    if (worldRank == 0) {\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(c, \"Concentration\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            bool valid = validateResult(c, nx, ny, nz);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // ---- Cleanup ----------------------------------------------------------\n    if (activeRank) {\n        if ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA implementation.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * MPI  : 1D domain decomposition along Z. Each rank owns a contiguous slab\n//            of Z planes and drives one GPU. A halo of 2 planes on each side is\n//            exchanged once per time step, which is enough to compute the\n//            chemical potential in the first ghost plane locally and therefore\n// constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Tile geometry for the stencil kernels\n#define TX 32\n#define TY 8\n\nenum StencilMode { MODE_MU = 0, MODE_UPDATE = 1 };\n\n// Fused tiled stencil kernel.\n//   MODE_MU     : out = chemical potential of \"in\" (== c)\n//   MODE_UPDATE : out = aux + dtD * laplacian(in) (in == mu, aux == c)\n//\n// Local Z indices run over [0, nzloc+4); owned planes are [2, nzloc+2).\n// The global Z coordinate of local plane k is gzoff + k, which drives the\n// clamped boundary condition at the physical domain boundaries.\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(TX* TY) void stencilKernel(const double* __restrict__ in, const int kbeg, const int kend, const int kchunk,\n                                                        const long gzoff, const long nzg, const double idx2,\n                                                        const double idy2, const double idz2, const double gamma,\n                                                        const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n                                                        const double dtD) {\n    __shared__ double s[TY + 2][TX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * static_cast<size_t>(min(yc + 1, ny - 1)) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n\n    double cm = in[static_cast<size_t>(k0 - 1) * plane + row + xc];\n    double c0 = in[static_cast<size_t>(k0) * plane + row + s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = c0;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[base + row + xm];\n        if (tx == TX - 1) s[ty + 1][TX + 1] = in[base + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[base +       const double dn = (gz > 0) ? cm : c0;\n\n        const double cxx = (s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * c0) * idx2;\n        const double cyy = (s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * c0) * idy2;\n        const double czz = (up + dn - 2.0 * c0) * idz2;\n        const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n        if (active) {\n            const size_t o = base + static_cast<size_t>(y) * static_cast<size_t>(nx) + static_cast<size_t>(x);\n            if (MODE == MODE_MU) {\n                out[o] = 4.5 * ((c0 + 1.0) * e_AA + (c0 - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * c0 * e_AB) + 3.0 * c0 + c0 * c0 *            const size_t z0, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nzloc; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n nz;\n\n    // ---- MPI decomposition along Z ---------------------------------------\n    // Every active rank needs at least 2 owned planes so that a 2-plane halo\n    // can be served by its cnt;\n            }\n            allCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(cnt * nx * ny);\n            allDispls[r] = static_cast<int>(off * nx * ny);\n            off += cnt;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---- Each rank owns a contiguous slab\n//            of Z planes and drives one GPU. A halo of 2 planes on each side is\n//            exchanged once per time step, which is enough to compute the\n//            chemical potential in the first ghost plane locally and therefore\n//            to evaluate the second (update) stencil on all owned cells.\n//   * CUDA : both stencil sweeps run as shared-memory tiled kernels that march\n//            along Z keeping the Z-neighbours in constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Tile geometry for the stencil kernels\n#define TX 32\n#define TY 8\n\nenum StencilMode { MODE_MU = 0, MODE_UPDATE = 1 };\n\n// Fused tiled stencil kernel.\n//   MODE_MU     : out = chemical potential of \"in\" (== c)\n//   MODE_UPDATE : out = aux + dtD * laplacian(in) (in == mu, aux == c)\n//\n// Local Z indices run over [0, nzloc+4); owned planes are [2, nzloc+2).\n// The global Z coordinate of local plane k is gzoff + k, which drives the\n// clamped boundary condition at the physical domain boundaries.\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(TX* TY) void stencilKernel(const double* __restrict__ in, const double* __restrict__ aux,\n                                                        double* __restrict__ out, const int nx, const int ny,\n                                                        const int kbeg, const int kend, const int kchunk,\n                                                        const long gzoff, const long nzg, const double idx2,\n                                                        const double idy2, const double idz2, const double gamma,\n                                                        const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n                                                        const double dtD) {\n    __shared__ double s[TY + 2][TX + 2];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * TX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * TY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still load (clamped) values so that they feed\n    // the correct halo entries to their in-range neighbours.\n    const int xc = min(x, nx - 1);\n    const int yc = min(y, ny - 1);\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(yc) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n\n    const int k0 = kbeg + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * kchunk;\n    const int k1 = min(k0 + kchunk, kend);\n    if (k0 >= k1) return;\n\n    const int xm = max(xc - 1, 0);\n    const int xp = min(xc + 1, nx - 1);\n    const size_t rowm = static_cast<size_t>(max(yc - 1, 0)) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n    const size_t rowp = static_cast<size_t>(min(yc + 1, ny - 1)) * static_cast<size_t>(nx);\n\n    double cm = in[static_cast<size_t>(k0 - 1) * plane + row + xc];\n    double c0 = in[static_cast<size_t>(k0) * plane + row + xc];\n\n    for (int k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n        const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(k) * plane;\n        const double cp = in[base + plane + row + xc];\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        s[ty + 1][tx + 1] = c0;\n        if (tx == 0) s[ty + 1][0] = in[base + row + xm];\n        if (tx == TX - 1) s[ty + 1][TX + 1] = in[base + row + xp];\n        if (ty == 0) s[0][tx + 1] = in[base + rowm + xc];\n        if (ty == TY - 1) s[TY + 1][tx + 1] = in[base + rowp + xc];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const long gz = gzoff + k;\n        const double up = (gz < nzg - 1) ? cp : c0;\n        const double dn = (gz > 0) ? cm : c0;\n\n        const double cxx = (s[ty + 1][tx + 2] + s[ty + 1][tx] - 2.0 * c0) * idx2;\n        const double cyy = (s[ty + 2][tx + 1] + s[ty][tx + 1] - 2.0 * c0) * idy2;\n        const double czz = (up + dn - 2.0 * c0) * idz2;\n        const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n        if (active) {\n            const size_t o = base + static_cast<size_t>(y) * static_cast<size_t>(nx) + static_cast<size_t>(x);\n            if (MODE == MODE_MU) {\n                out[o] = 4.5 * ((c0 + 1.0) * e_AA + (c0 - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * c0 * e_AB) + 3.0 * c0 + c0 * c0 * Initialize the local slab of the concentration field (host, OpenMP threaded)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t z0, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nzloc; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = 1;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\",    }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (worldRank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_sendHi, 2 * plane * sizeof(double)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recvLo, 2 * plane * sizeof(double)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recvHi, 2 * Each rank owns a contiguous slab\n//            of Z planes and drives one GPU. A halo of 2 planes on each side is\n//            exchanged once per time step, which is enough to compute the\n//            chemical potential in the first ghost plane locally and therefore\n//            to evaluate the second (update) stencil on all owned cells.\n//   * CUDA : both stencil sweeps run as barrier-free kernels that march along Z\n//            keeping the Z-neighbours in registers and obtaining the\n//            X-neighbours through warp shuffles.\n//   * OpenMP: host side work (field initialization, validation reductions,\n//            halo staging) is threaded.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                               \\\n    do {                                                                                                               \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                               \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                                     \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);                  \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                              \\\n        }                                                                                                              \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Tile geometry for the stencil kernels\n#define TX 32\n#define TY 8\n\nstatic_assert(TX == 32, \"the x-neighbour warp shuffles require a 32-wide tile\");\n\nenum StencilMode { MODE_MU = 0, MODE_UPDATE = 1 };\n\n// Fused tiled stencil kernel.\n//   MODE_MU     : out = chemical potential of \"in\" (== c)\n//   MODE_UPDATE : out = aux + dtD * laplacian(in) (in == mu, aux == c)\n//\n// Local Z indices run over [0, nzloc+4); owned planes are [2, nzloc+2).\n// The global Z coordinate of local plane k is gzoff + k, which drives the\n// clamped boundary condition at the physical domain boundaries.\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(TX* TY) void stencilKernel(const double* __restrict__ in, const double* __restrict__ aux,\n                                                        double* __restrict__ out, const int nx, const int ny,\n                                                        const int kbeg, const int kend, const int kchunk,\n                                                        const long gzoff, const long nzg, const double idx2,\n                                                        const double idy2, const double idz2, const double gamma,\n                                                        const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n                                                        const double dtD) {\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * TX + tx;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * TY + ty;\n\n    // Threads outside the domain still evaluate (clamped) coordinates so that\n    // they feed the correct halo values to their in-range neighbours.\n    const int xc = min(x, nx - 1);\n    const int yc = min(y, ny - 1);\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    const size_t plane = (size_t)nx * (size_t)ny;\n    const size_t row = (size_t)yc * (size_t)nx;\n\n    const int k0 = kbeg + (int)blockIdx.z * kchunk;\n    const int k1 = min(k0 + kchunk, kend);\n    if (k0 >= k1) return;\n\n    const int xm = max(xc - 1, 0);\n    const int xp = min(xc + 1, nx - 1);\n    const size_t rowm = (size_t)max(yc - 1, 0) * (size_t)nx;\n    const size_t rowp = (size_t)min(yc + 1, ny - 1) * (size_t)nx;\n\n    double cm = in[(size_t)(k0 - 1) * plane + row + xc];\n    double c0 = in[(size_t)k0 * plane + row + xc];\n\n    for (int k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)k * plane;\n        const double cp = in[base + plane + row + xc];\n        // x-neighbours come from the warp (one warp == one row of 32 cells),\n        // y-neighbours from L1/L2, z-neighbours from the sweep registers.\n        double wl = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n        double wr = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n        if (tx == 0) wl = in[base + row + xm];\n        if (tx == TX - 1) wr = in[base + row + xp];\n        const double yl = in[base + rowm + xc];\n        const double yr = in[base + rowp + xc];\n\n        const long gz = gzoff + k;\n        const double up = (gz < nzg - 1) ? cp : c0;\n        const double dn = (gz > 0) ? cm : c0;\n        const double two_c0 = 2.0 * c0;\n        const double cxx = (wr + wl - two_c0) * idx2;\n        const double cyy = (yr + yl - two_c0) * idy2;\n        const double czz = (up + dn - two_c0) * idz2;\n        const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n        if (active) {\n            const size_t o = base + (size_t)y * (size_t)nx + (size_t)x;\n            if (MODE == MODE_MU) {\n                out[o] = 4.5 * ((c0 + 1.0) * e_AA + (c0 - 1.0) * e_BB - two_c0 * e_AB) + 3.0 * c0 + c0 * c0 * c0 -\n                         gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[o] = aux[o] + dtD * lap;\n            }\n        }\n        cm = c0;\n        c0 = cp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize the local slab of the concentration field (host, OpenMP threaded)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t z0, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nzloc; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = (z + z0) * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = 1;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int worldRank = 0;\n    int worldSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);\n\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (worldRank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (worldRank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (worldRank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cej6jNX7mcUZH7QfedJAY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. Summary of the work:\n\n## Approach\n\n**MPI** — 1D domain decomposition along Z, one rank per GPU (round-robin over devices within each node via `MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED`). Each rank keeps a 2-plane ghost region on each side. Depth 2 is the key choice: it lets each rank compute the chemical potential in its first ghost plane locally, so the two chained Laplacians per time step need only **one** halo exchange instead of two.\n\n**CUDA** — both sweeps use one templated barrier-free kernel (`stencilKernel<MODE_MU|MODE_UPDATE>`). Each thread owns an (x,y) column and marches along Z, carrying the z-neighbours in registers; x-neighbours come from warp shuffles (one warp = one 32-cell row), y-neighbours from L1/L2. Out-of-range threads still compute clamped coordinates so they feed correct boundary values to their in-range neighbours — the clamped BC falls out for free. Z chunking per block is sized from the SM count so small grids still fill the GPU.\n\n**Overlap** — the loop is software-pipelined. Interior `mu` (which depends only on owned planes) runs on stream 1 while the previous step's non-blocking halo exchange is still in flight; the interior update runs while the outgoing exchange is posted. Boundary and interior phases are ordered with two CUDA events. The code uses CUDA-aware MPI when the runtime advertises it, otherwise pinned host staging.\n\n**OpenMP** — host-side field initialization and the validation reductions.\n\n## Correctness\n\nBit-exact against the original serial code (identical `Sum`/`Min`/`Max`/samples/**hash**) across 1–5 ranks for grids including 64³, 37×51×29, 128×64×96, 3×1×2 and 1³. `--fmad=false` keeps device arithmetic in the source's unfused evaluation order.\n\nOne thing to flag: the *stock* build of the original (`-O3 -march=native`) is **not** bit-identical to this — GCC contracts FMAs by default there, so its hash differs from the unfused result by ~1e-16 relative. This port matches the original compiled with `-ffp-contract=off`, i.e. the arithmetic the source actually specifies. Matching GCC's particular fusion choices on a GPU isn't achievable, so I optimized for source-faithful semantics.\n\n## Performance (512³, 50 steps, RTX 3090)\n\n| config | time | MCellUpdates/s |\n|---|---|---|\n| original (1 CPU core) | ~115 s | ~58 |\n| 1 GPU | 1044 ms | 6 428 |\n| 2 GPUs | 559 ms | 12 005 |\n| 4 GPUs | 330 ms | 20 336 |\n\n3.2× on 4 GPUs (pure per-GPU compute for a 1/4 slab is 281 ms, so exchange overhead is down to ~0.9 ms/step from ~4.3 ms before pipelining), ~350× over the original. Single-GPU is FP64-throughput bound at ~48% of the 3090's FP64 peak — block shape sweeps (32×4 … 128×2) all landed within 3%.\n\nOnly `cahn_hilliard.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt` were changed; the executable name and CLI are unchanged, and no new dependencies were added."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4136,"cache_read_input_tokens":99051,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4136},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0880e60e-b9a1-44e7-9f40-3903ea14f6ee","uuid":"fddb3b98-b66c-442c-a7b7-c5f65c23ae73","timestamp":"2026-09-04T21:21:25.070Z","request_id":"req_011Cej6jMNfHt8HZ71Akcork"}
